UEW Card Archive

Holiday Horror 3

circa January 1999
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
(Fade into an exterior shot of the Tokyo Dome. The camera pans around giving a full view of the Tokyo Dome from above. Finally, the camera focuses on the sign in front of the building. The electronic board is flashing messages in Japanese, followed by the American translations. The board changes to read TONIGHT ONLY: Ultimate E-Wrestling proudly presents HOLIDAY HORROR 3!!!) Voice over: Tonight, from a sold out Tokyo Dome, the UEW will present Holiday Horror. Tonight, the waiting ends. For months, 'Ladykiller' Chris Tyler has dominated the competition. He has wiped out every obstacle placed on his path to his ultimate goal. Now only one man stands between the Ladykiller and the Ultimate title. Tonight, Chris Tyler will face the Top Dog, Rick Styles. Rick Styles, the leader of the New Alliance and 4-time Ultimate champion stands a top of the wrestling world... but for how long? Will Chris Tyler dethrone the champion? Or will the Top Dog claim another victim? (A picture is shown of Chris Tyler and Rick Styles with the Ultimate title in-between them.) VO: Tonight, 4 teams who have traveled vastly different paths will collide. The Explosion, holders of both the Ultimate and Television titles rule the tag division. The Perfectly Perfect Duo, representing the New Alliance, have had an impressive stay in UEW, but look to add their first UEW title to their already impressive resume. The Partners in Crime feel that they have been overlooked by the championship committee in the past, but finally have their opportunity to claim gold. The Partners are hoping to steal the gold here tonight. But, the promising young team known as Power and Honor will be looking to start a Revolution in the tag division. This team has defeated every opponent to date, and looks to keep that run going and bring home the gold for the fledgling Revolution stable. (A shot of all four teams inside the Heaven and Hell cage appears. On the mat, one wrestler from each team is seen grabbing for the Ultimate title, while above, one member for each team is seen reaching for the Television title) HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHHHHHHHHH HHHHHHHHHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHHHHHHHHH HHHHHHHHHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH HHHHH 3333333333333333 3333333333333333 33333 33333 3333333333 3333333333 33333 33333 3333333333333333 3333333333333333 (fade to black) ###################################################### ### ########## #### ### ######## ### ### ########## #### ########### ######## ### ### ########## #### ########### ######## ### ### ########## #### ########### ######## ### ### ########## #### #### ## ## ### ### ########## #### ########### # ## # ### #### ######## ##### ########### #### ### ##### ###### ###### ########### ###### ### ####### ######## ### ######## ### ###################################################### ********************* ***_W_A_R_N_I_N_G_*** ********************* What you are about to witness is the ULTIMATE in e-wrestling entertainment. The action you are about to see is done by professionals in every sense of the word. Do not try this at home, or in your own fed..... You should not shield your eyes, nor change the channel...... For this is the ULTIMATE, and it doesn't get any better than this. Enjoy the presentation. [fade to black] (The opening graphics give way to a slow pan of the interior of the Tokyo Dome. It is a breath taking view. Everywhere, there are fans standing in anticipation. Some where T-shirts or other UEW paraphernalia. There doesn't seem to be any particular favorites amongst the early shots, but rather almost all of the UEW stars seem to have merchandise seen. As the shot pans down near ringside, the scene is vastly different. Right at ringside, the first few rows are lined with a loud, rowdy bunch of American fans. In this area, the favorites are clear... "Ladykiller" Chris Tyler and "Mr. Excitement" Alex Extreme. The camera pans around further, and then backs out to give us a wide view of the whole arena once more. The ring sits in the center of the arena. Above, their are two scaffolds. One, is a smaller one with lights hanging from it and other technical items. The other, is a larger one. Easily recognizable as the scaffold for the Heaven and Hell match. Another distinguishing feature is a long runway. Perhaps as long as a football field between the ring and the back area. This building is absolutely huge, and this runway only accentuates that fact. That, and the tens of thousands of fans that line the arena. Finally, the camera pans upwards once again, and we get exterior shots of the skyboxes that ring the upper part of the arena. When the camera pans down again, it settles on the UEW announce platform, located off to the side of the runway. We see Magnus Colby and Chris Flint standing there. Both men wear full tuxedos for the occasion.) Chris Flint: Ladies and gentlemen, welcome to Holiday Horror 3. We are live via Pay-Per-View from the Tokyo Dome in Japan. We are here to bring you a night that will truly display why we are indeed the Ultimate in E-Wrestling today. I will be here to cover the action tonight as always, and with me for the evening is former UEW American champion, Magnus Colby. Magnus Colby: Chris, I cannot tell you how happy I am to be here. I know in the past, I was one of those who argued to keep the UEW stateside because I don't like traveling internationally. Well, I am here to tell you I was dead wrong. This week has been one of the best of my life. The locals have been just wonderful. Flint: Yes they have. You were here before most of the UEW got here Magnus. Tell me what the word is on the streets. Colby: Well Chris, the whole town has been buzzing about the arrival of the UEW on the island. And let me tell you, they bring that excitement to the arena tonight. Hours before the show was set to start, they were lined up waiting to see this thing get underway. Flint: Is there any one match that the fans are talking about, or are they just looking forward to the whole thing? Colby: Well, like any other UEW event, their is a great level of anticipation for the whole card. That said, the Japanese fans like good old fashioned mat wrestling. For the most part, they don't like gimmick matches... so, that points squarely at one match. The main event, the one we've all been looking forward to. Flint: Of course, the big title defense for Rick Styles and he takes on "the Ladykiller" Chris Tyler. Well, this looks like it is going to be one heck of a card. Why don't we start right from the top with that main event? Colby: Let's. In our main event, we will see 4-time UEW Ultimate champion and leader of the New Alliance, "Top Dog" Rick Styles defend his title against the number one rated wrestler in UEW history. That man is of course, "Ladykiller" Chris Tyler. Flint: Chris claims he will win here tonight, and it will be him to lead the UEW into the 21st century. The UEW faithful have gotten squarely behind Chris the last couple of months as he has straightened up his act, and learned not to take the easy way out all the time. Colby: That's right Chris, Tyler has become a role model for all the kids out there. His relentless pursuit of success is admirable. But, it's going to take everything he's got to beat the man standing across the ring from him tonight. Make no mistake, there is a reason why Rick Styles is standing a top the UEW for a fourth time. He's quite simply the greatest UEW wrestler of all time. Flint: Well Magnus, that's high praise, and certainly to date, the Top Dog has lived up to that billing. But, the possibility remains that Chris Tyler could knock him off here tonight. With that win, I think Tyler would have to be considered amongst the greatest ever as well. Colby: No doubt. This match should be one for the ages. From the Ultimate in singles wrestling, we go to the Ultimate in tag wrestling. Last year at Holiday Horror 2, the UEW rolled out another original match. Throw 4 teams in a cage, hang a scaffold from above it, and throw two sets of tag title into the mix, and what do you get? Flint: You get Heaven and Hell. Tonight, we bring you a second Heaven and Hell match. Amazingly, only one team returns from last years event. Meaning, that in just 12 months, the UEW's tag division has completely turned over. Colby: Sometimes change is for the best Chris, and I think right now the UEW tag division is just about as strong as it has ever been. For most of 98, the Outlaws ran over all the competition in UEW. They were seemingly unstoppable ... unstoppable that is until the team self destructed. In the wake of that breakup, the Explosion captured the Ultimate titles for the first time. Flint: That's right, and that was only a short time after they ended the Television title reign of the Lethal Leaders. The Explosion walk into tonight's matchup with all the gold. They are also that one team that returns from last year. Colby: That experience could be vital in this one. This matchup is as wild as they get, especially when you consider the people involved in it. The Explosion are not exactly known for their love of the rule book, but neither are many of their opponents. One of the teams, the Partners in Crime, are perhaps most known for the chairs that they routinely carry to ringside. Flint: Well, chairs they carried to ringside until they were stolen. Allegedly by the Explosion themselves. The Partners routinely throw the rule book out the window and rely on good old fashioned brawling to win matches. That could serve them well in this match. Colby: It sure could Chris, but if this thing disintegrates into a brawl, I have to like the chances of the Perfectly Perfect Duo. They represent the New Alliance, and therefore, they train with some of the dirtiest players the game has ever seen. I know Rick Styles, and I know the Duo's manager, Sensuous Samantha, and I can tell you the Duo will have some trick up their sleeves that will pull this one off for them. Flint: You could be right, the wild card in this whole thing has to be the one team that not only will be trying to bring order to this match, but to the whole UEW. Colby: That's a large task, but one I think the team you are talking about, Power and Honor is capable of carrying out. These two men have been extremely impressive since their debut match... an Ultimate title shot against the Outlaws. Flint: That's right, that night they came within an eyelash of securing the Ultimate titles in just their first UEW tag match. That night, many predicted a long a glorious future in UEW for these two, and nothing has happened to change that outlook. Power and Honor are a force to be reckoned with. Colby: Well, right now the Explosion sit a top the tag world in UEW... but for how long? That is the question we will look to see answered here tonight. Flint: Another question many UEW fans have been asking is can former UEW Ultimate champion Rob Magnum return to form and capture the American title here tonight? Colby: Well Chris, that is an awfully good question. The only thing I can tell you is that when he is on his game, Rob Magnum is one of the toughest wrestlers in the world. My question for you though, is can Magnum even in top form beat the current American champion? Flint: Well, Nevermind owns some very impressive victories, but so does Magnum. That is a question we will all look to see answered here tonight. Colby: I'll never understand why it is that people continue to overlook Nevermind. The man boasts one of the best records in the history of UEW. He has never been pinned, and his only loss was a match he walked away from. He's beaten the seemingly unbeatable Chris Tyler. He's beaten me... just weeks before I would become American champion. He's beaten Creed, a man tabbed by everyone as a future Ultimate title contender. What does this man have to do to get the respect of the UEW fans? Flint: Well, in reality, he probably needs a total attitude adjustment. He racks up the victories, but his style is sometimes lackluster, and maybe people feel he is just in the right place at the right time. I know one thing for sure, as long as he keeps racking up victories, respect is a moot point. He doesn't want it, and respect doesn't pay the bills. Colby: Excellent point Chris. I guess if he doesn't care, I shouldn't either. I'll tell you though, I know one man who will be watching the American title match intently though. I'm of course talking about "the Black Knight" Alex Martinez. Alex was stripped of a title shot on the last Sunday Night Heat. Alex has appealed the UEW championship committee for another shot. Flint: Well, I am sure it is only a matter of time before Alex gets his wish and gets that title shot. But, he has bigger worries here tonight. He goes into a match that is easily the most dangerous in UEW history. The Ultimate Death Match. I need no further explanation of how dangerous that match is then take one look at how pale you get at the very mention of the match. Colby: Well, I can't hide from the truth. I fought in the original Ultimate Death Match, and through the treachery of Kyle Kamikaze, I suffered an injury that at least temporarily ended my UEW career. That's not to take anything away from Alex Martinez, the man who was in there doing the damage, but I was fighting against interference from Kamikaze. Flint: Well, Kyle paid the price for his actions. He was fired from the UEW on that date, and can no longer wrestle in the UEW. Tonight, Alex Martinez will step into the second Ultimate Death match, and this time he will be looking across the ring at the number two contender to the Ultimate title. Mr. Excitement himself, Alex Extreme. Colby: I just hope Extreme is 100 percent going into this match. The attack by Martinez on the last Sunday Night Heat that prevented Extreme from getting his Ultimate title shot, has left the extreme one hospitalized since then. Flint: Somehow, I doubt anything will stop Extreme from getting in the ring tonight. He has revenge on the brain, and what better match to get revenge in then an Ultimate Death match? Colby: Revenge is not a goal in a death match. Survival is a goal. If Extreme goes in there trying to even up a score, Martinez will do worse than cripple him. He'll kill him. Remember, I had about 70 pounds of muscle more than Extreme does to absorb those blows from the giant Martinez. If this one gets into a slugfest, we could see the end of Extreme. Flint: We shall see. We shall also see a four way bout for the Television title. The current champion, Johnny Glamour, will defend his title against three of the UEW's top stars. Colby: Hate to interrupt you, but what is Johnny's gimmick this week? That man changes gimmicks more often than some people change underwear. Flint: You're just upset because he attacked you in some other federation. Please refrain from showing your bias. Colby: Whatever, it's not like he's the only one in this match I don't care much for. I mean, that piece of trash Liam Rogan is a part of this match as well. Flint: Trash? What kind of journalism is that? You are talking about one of the top rated wrestlers in the UEW, and the number one contender to the Television title. Colby: Big deal, Rogan is lower than the scum on the bottom of my boots. As such, he will get stomped by the two men I do like in this match. Steve Spector and Mannifred Lester. Flint: Don't you mean Shimmer Glitz? Colby: No, I mean Lester. Les and I have been friends for a long time. Mannifred Lester is one of the most dangerous men in UEW history, and a former Ultimate champion. Flint: Mannifred Lester has left the UEW. Shimmer Glitz has replaced him. Colby: Shimmer Glitz is a freak. If Les is gonna insist on maintaining that charade, then I am going to have to support Steve Spector. The one voice of sanity in this match. Steve is one tough customer, and perhaps the only true role model in the UEW today. Flint: Finally, you say something I can agree on. Steve Spector does things the right way... the old fashioned way. He takes no short cuts. He plays by the rules, and gets whatever he gets through hard work and dedication. I would be proud to have Steve Spector representing the UEW as one of it's champions. Colby: And finally, you are making sense. I might refrain from killing you tonight after all. In another exciting match tonight, we will see "the Nighthawk" Nick Leshenn take on "Too Cool" Chris Hopper. Flint: Hopper, once regarded as on of the top fan favorites in the game, has turned his back on the fans. Colby: His fans? What about his former best friend Alex Extreme? Chris attacked Alex in an attempt to keep him from winning War Games at November Nightmare, then last week he actually attempted to kill him by throwing him off of a bridge. Flint: Well, something is wrong with Hopper. Those actions are so unlike him. Maybe, just maybe Nick Leshenn can straighten him out here tonight. Thank God Nick was able to stop Chris from throwing Extreme off that bridge last week. Chris Hopper has seemingly lost his mind. Colby: Well, he had better have his head screwed on straight when he steps into the ring with Leshenn, or Nick will knock it right off. Then he won't have anything to worry about at all. Flint: Another wild match tonight, will pit members of the old New Foundation, Ivan Kolinski and Professor Payne, against members of the old Widow Makers Inc., Marcus Manson and the Mercenary. Colby: Well, everyone in this match has held a UEW Ultimate title. The advantage seemingly lies in the fact that Marcus Manson's was a tag title. This match is a tag match, and that could be to his advantage. Flint: Well, I'd agree with you that this match favors the Widow Maker team, but I see it a little different. This is really not a tag match, but a 4 man brawl. And, in a brawl I favor the Mercenary to come out on top. Colby: Another great point, but on the other side is one of the most talented teams in UEW history Nick Payne and Ivan Kolinski are two of the greatest wrestlers in the history of the UEW. They will be no pushover in this match. That much I can guarantee. I just don't think a street fight bodes well for them. Flint: Agreed, but Professor Payne always seems to have a trick up his sleeve. Maybe tonight, he can pull another ace out of the hole. Colby: Speaking of tricks, let's talk about Bag of Tricks. The man who invented the Bag of Tricks match, and participated in the original, was J'ster. This two time former Television champion will be looking to knock off the man who claims to be the new jester in town. Flint: The Trixxster is a promising young UEW star. I think he'll be able to score the victory over J'ster in this one. I just don't know where J'ster's head is after turning on the New Alliance several weeks back. Colby: No, I can't question his motives for leaving them, but I do know what you are talking about. J'ster has admittedly had some personal problems, and I am not sure he is prepared to separate his inner turmoil from this match. He is just not in the proper mindset to take on an opponent the caliber of Trixxster in a match like this. Flint: Well, Magnus, one match we have neglected to talk about so far, is an explosive one. Revolution member Creed, will take on former member "Real Deal" Shawn Harrison. Colby: Well, we all know how I feel about Creed. I think he is a future UEW star. But, Harrison does have more ring experience than Creed. In a match of this magnitude, I think Harrison might be able to pull this one off. Flint: I couldn't agree more. Harrison is wrestling with a chip on his shoulder. For years, Harrison lived in the shadow of former UEW star, Jeff "Madfox" Matthews. Finally, Harrison has escaped from that shadow only to step into that of Creed. Only with a victory here tonight can Shawn finally free himself from the shadows and step into the spotlights he so desperately craves. Colby: That one should be incredible. We will also see the culmination of the Colby Cup here tonight. We started nearly two months ago with 16 men. Tonight, we have just 4 remaining. Four men competing for a shot at the American title and 100 grand. That's a lot of cash. Flint: The two semifinal matches will pit "the Puppet Master" Nicolas Christian against Doomsday, and "Devil's Advocate" Bryan Sullivan against "Drifter" J.D. Carter. Colby: If you'd asked me when I originally signed the 16 competitors, who the 4 men we'd be seeing here tonight, I'd have told you Sullivan would be here, but even I am surprised by the performances that brought the other 3 here. Flint: Well, Christian took the UEW by surprise, stunning Prince Judife here in the first round, and then followed that up with another amazing victory over the Machine Chris K. Both of those men were considered favorites going into this tournament, but the current talk has Christian continuing his Cinderella run towards the top, and in the process knocking off Doomsday. Colby: Well, Doomsday beat Scott Haggarty, another man I expected to be here tonight as part of the Colby Cup. He followed that up with a victory over fellow upset specialist, Johnny Bod. Flint: In the other bracket, we saw Bryan Sullivan score a victory over the CandyMan, and J.D. Carter defeat "the Natural Born Killer" Patrick Alexander. Both were impressive victories. Give us your thoughts on these two semifinal matches. Colby: Well, in the first one, I think the end of the line had arrived for Doomsday. Doomsday is a dangerous man, but I just think Nicolas Christian is in the zone right now, so to speak. I think he can parlay his momentum into a victory and a trip to the finals. In the other match, I think Carter should pose a difficult challenge for Sullivan. Carter's style has caught his two previous opponents off guard, and I am not sure Sullivan can adapt quick enough. Flint: Well, I have to disagree with you there, I think Bryan can and will get past the Drifter. Either way, I think you, me, and all the other so called experts are expecting a Nicolas Christian victory in the finals regardless of the opponent. Colby: I'd love to disagree with you, but the man seems unstoppable. I can't help thinking he reminds me of a young Chris Tyler with the energy and excitement he brings to the ring night in and night out. Flint: High praise being considered in that mold. Christian though probably doesn't mind. I think he is destined for greatness... but, first he needs to score two victories here tonight. Colby: Well, that just leaves one match to talk about. The big 15 man cruiser weight battle royal for the E.C.G.P.I. Cruiser cup. Some of the favorites in this match include former Outlaw Doc Holliday, Al Robertson, V.D. Vitrone, and Patrick Alexander. Flint: Don't forget Johnny Bod, and the current Cruiser Cup champ, Jonathan Witt. Why don't we just go ahead and send this one down to ringside to get things underway? Colby: That sounds like a wonderful idea. With that, we send it down to the ring, and Holiday Horror 2 _is_ _under_ _way_ (Suddenly, a voice interrupts the announcers...) Voice: Hey, now! This' MAH show, an' ah'll git it started right! HELLO TOKYO! (The normally reserved Japanese crowd BOOOOOs the hated sound of Doc Holliday's voice. He's well-known here, and the last time he wrestled in the Tokyo Dome, earlier this month, he didn't make any friends... A spotlight blazes up into the skybox area, where the high-prices luxury suites for VIPs are. A window to one is open, and Doc Holliday is standing in it with a house mic, waving mockingly to all the fans. He's dressed in a fine black suit, with an old-style ruffled undershirt. A black Stetson finishes the attire.) Holliday: My, but y'all look lak ants alla way down thar! Oh, wait... ah forgot, this is Japan, y'all look lak ants up close too! (BOOOO!) Holliday: Now, since ah own this heah show... an' twenty percent o' all th' money ya spent on yer tickets went right direct ta mah pocket... ah suppose ah should give a right proper introduction! Welcome... TO DOC HOLLIDAY HORROR 3!!!! (BOOOO! This is very unusual for a Japanese crowd... they're acting much like an American crowd tonight. That, and they all hate Holliday with a passion.) Now, ah had a lotta changes thet woulda made this heah card th' greatest spectacle evah seen! But, UEW wasn't gonna let it happen... though... they DID make some... compromises... heh heh heh. Anyway, ah kin assure ya thet tonight, ah'll be watchin' up heah, with a close eye on EVER'THANG... an' ah do mean EVER'THANG... thet goes on! An' when mah Main Event match comes around an' ah take ta whoopin' hell outta 14 yahoos, ah bet you'd all really lak it if'n Brent Maverick showed his weak ass up, wouldn't ya! Well... ... ah'm sorry ta say... (Doc gets a sad expression on his face, and takes off his hat and holds it in front of his heart.) ... Brent Maverick is dead. (GASP!) Chris Flint: WHAT?! Holliday: Yup, th' snake poison finally got to 'im. See, las' night aftah th' Extry Dark, his doctors were warnin' 'im about how he hadda git treatment, or th' toxin cyclin' syndrome'd send thet pent-up venom in his liver back through his bloodstream the first time he had a big shock. An' well, he wouldn't lissen ta th' doctor... so I hit him inna head with a garbage can so he'd pay attention! Ha haaa... anyway, aftah ah hit 'im a couple'a times, he done fell down an' died. Ain't thet sad? (BOOOOOOOO!!) Colby: I had heard that an unknown assailant had attacked Brent Maverick after the Extra Dark card yesterday folks, but I cannot, I repeat, cannot confirm nor deny what Holliday is claiming right now. Holliday: Hey, now, don' blame me! Ah ain't legally liable fer his death! He was th' one who left a hospital outside o' doctor's orders! He was disobeyin' doctor's orders, an' if anyone goes ta prison fer him dyin', it'll be Explosion... they's th' ones thet poisoned 'im, not me! But, all thet mattahs is he's dead, an' we gotta let his name rest in peace! SO NOBODY TALK ABOUT BRENT MAVERICK NO' MO'! They's only ONE TRUE OUTLAW in UEW, an' yer all lookin' at him! Enjoy the show. (The shot fades back to the broadcast booth, where Magnus Colby and Chris Flint are both stunned.) Flint: Well, that certainly puts a downer on the show. Folks, I am told we have dispatched Josh Taylor to look into the situation, I promise you we will update the situation as soon as we have more information. Colby: Well, Brent would certainly want us to continue with business as usual, so let's get things rolling with the cruiser battle royal. (Suddenly, "Battlestar Galactica" begins to play throughout the Tokyo Dome. Rick Styles walks up the aisle dressed in a red New Alliance jacket, his wrestling tights and the UEW Ultimate Championship around his waist. Walking to the left of the champion is Guido, who keeps the fans from reaching over the guardrail and grabbing at Styles' jacket.) -------------------------------------------------------------------------- -------------------- Words of wisdom from the Top Dog plus the battle royal in part 2!!! Subj: [UEW] HOLIDAY HORROR 3 (part 2) Date: 12/27/98 11:15:23 PM Central Standard Time From: MAGCOLBY CC: UEWPREZ Flint: The reigning UEW Ultimate Champion makes his way down that LONG aisle which leads towards the ring. A walk I'm sure he's not overly accustomed to. Colby: But the boos from the crowd, he is accustomed to, even they are in Japanese. Flint: Actually, most of the jeers are coming from the first four rows filled with loyal UEW fans who attend our weekly shows on a continual basis. Tickets and airfare, our way of saying thank you and Happy Holidays from the UEW. Colby: Don't forget to mention that I was the one who financed that appreciation package. Flint: How can I? You never let us forget. Rick Styles has finally made his way into the ring. Rick "the ring announcer" hands him a microphone. He's not here for the Main Event this early, I assume. Colby: Don't be stupid on international Television, Chris. (Styles twirls the microphone in his hand as he waits for the crowd to settle down. He licks his lips and places the microphone up towards his mouth) Rick Styles: Tokyo, Japan! Land of the Rising Sun and the Fish Capital of the World!! (A chorus of boos are heard throughout the arena while a few of the American fans offer loud boos and throw empty cups and other garbage into the ring. Styles looks towards the rowdy group in the front and holds his microphone out towards them) Rick Styles: Listen to those boos!! MUSIC TO MY EARS!! Now, THAT is a legitimate crowd reaction! (looks down at the young teenager in the second row who threw one of the cups) Of course, this illiterate kid right here is the EXACT stereotype that you people think every American is like! (The teenager stands on his chair as if he were a celebrity and raises a middle finger towards Styles) Rick Styles: Heh, typical Chris Tyler fan right there. How proud your parents must be right now watching at home. Now how about the rest of you? First of all, how many of you can even understand what I'm saying right now? Comprende English anyone? Saki Saki, Five Dollar? (Another chorus of boos echoes throughout the Tokyo Dome. One Japanese fan in the 6th row stands up, holds a sign over his head with one hand and points at it with his other hand. The sign has Japanese letters on it. Styles points towards the sign and laughs.) Rick Styles: Hey, is that directed towards little ol' ME? Quick news flash, Hashimoto, I don't read Japanese! For all I know, that could say "Eat at Joes after the event"! And for all I care!! Now, the reason I'm out here is to bring up a statement recently made my opponent tonight. Chris Tyler has recently TRIED to coin a phrase called "Dog Dick" Logic. He CLAIMS this is a statement about yours truly, but when you think about it, What is Chris Tyler? He's a dog who thinks with his dick and makes illogical arrogant statements, yet claims they are indeed "logical." But the fact is, the ONLY thing about Chris Tyler's statements that even remotely resembles logic is the fact that they're being said by a guy with pointed ears. Let's compare Tyler ramblings to the TRUTH, shall we? (Styles raises a hand in the air with one finger stretched out) Rick Styles: Tyler states that the New Alliance wants DESPERATELY to have him as a member! The truth shows that we could have HAD Chris Tyler as a member of the NA, but WE didn't want HIM! (Styles holds a second finger out) Rick Styles: Chris Tyler laughs about my hiring phony Chris Tyler fans and filling an auditorium with them. That truth shows us that the "Universal Matinee Idol" needs to open his eyes more because if he did, he would have seen that no actor would play a Chris Tyler fan since it would require lowering themselves to a role just underneath playing a rock. THE ROOM WAS EMPTY, MORON!! DUH? (Styles holds out a third finger) Rick Styles: Most importantly, Chris Tyler has claimed on more then one occasion that he IS the current UEW Ultimate Champion! The truth is, as of right now, that belt sits firmly around my waist!! Now...who is correct? Who's truth is reality? To put it bluntly, the winner tonight writes the history books! And to the victor goes the spoils...the title and the ENTIRE U-E-W FRANCHISE!! (Styles looks around the arena towards the jeering fans) Rick Styles: So, tonight...Boo me, Jeer me and scream my name all you want!! But mostly get your hopes up REAL high by cheering on Chris Tyler in his pursuit of the UEW Ultimate Championship!! Because I'm going to drop a bomb right onto all of your and his hopes and dreams like none of you have EVER seen before...and it'll make those little firecrackers that went off in Hiroshima and Nagasaki 50 years ago PALE in comparison!! R"tR"A: Ladies and gentlemen... (Rick continues to speak, but the mic suddenly goes dead. Meanwhile, Rick Styles has released the hold, and has gotten up. J'ster is in the ring, and the two men are hugging as the other members of the New Alliance begin to stream out from the back. Kyle Kamikaze, Alex Martinez with his American title, and the Perfectly Perfect Duo with their tag belts make their way out to the ring area accompanied by all the various New Alliance personnel. Rick again tries to speak, but the mic is completely dead. He leans out of the ring and asks Kevin Diamond if he knows what the problem is, but Kevin just shrugs. Suddenly, Magnus Colby reappears at ringside. He is carrying a mic and a brief case.) Colby: You can test that mic all you want boys, but you will never get it to work. I am the only one who will speak at the moment. (Colby rolls into the ring. All of the members of the New Alliance look at him with hatred in their eyes. Chris Tyler looks on with a questioning gaze.) Colby: Before I get this meeting started, I'd like to invite a few people out to ringside to hear first hand what I have to say. Jay Steele, Mike Haynes, Don Thomas... get your asses out here to ringside. NOW!!! (The crowd murmurs and the New Alliance members laugh. The general consensus is that Magnus has completely flipped. Slowly, the UEW officials make their way out to ringside and climb into the ring. Jay Steele grabs the mic from Colby's hand.) Steele: Just what in God's name do you think you are doing? I gave you a front office position because you were too chicken to get back into the ring. But, I told you this is still my show. Don't you ever call me out in front of these people again. You got it? (Colby grabs the mic away from Steele again.) Colby: What I am doing is trying to save what little respect this federation has left. Steele: You are out of line Colby. I'm warning you. Shut up and get out of my ring or I will be forced to fire you. Colby: No Jay, it is you who is out of line. You have been out of line for a long time. I have sat in the shadows and watched. I have kept my mouth shut. Week after week, I have watched one match after another end in interference. I have seen referees blow calls. This isn't the NFL Jay. We should be able to count on the guys in the ring arbitrating these matches. We can't and you know it. Steele: So what do you propose I do about it? Colby: First of all, try being a president instead of hiding behind a desk all the time. Jay, you used to be the best president in the world. Everyone felt that... myself included. But, the times have passed you by. I have stood by and watched 4 titles change hands here tonight. Not one of those matches had a clean ending. There are a whole bunch of men in the back who lost in some cases through no fault of their own. Steele: Oh like you weren't one of the guys who did all the interfering? Colby: Oh yeah I was. I don't forget. I caused more havoc than I'd like to admit to. I have apologized to as many of them as I could find to apologize to. But, what's done it done. What matters is the future of UEW. A future I want all of us to be proud of. A future without Jay Steele. (The building grows deathly silent. Everyone knows now that Magnus has gone over the edge.) Steele: That's it. You are fired. (He turns to Don Thomas.) This man is never to be allowed in a UEW building again. (Colby yanks the mic away from Steele again.) Colby: No Steele, YOU are fired!!! Steele: On who's authority? Colby: On MY authority! Steele: Who died and left you king? Colby: No one. (He opens up his briefcase and pulls out some documents. He hands them to Steele.) You will find in that pamphlet, bill of sales for enough shares to make me the majority owner of the UEW. You will also find a buyout offer on your contract. You have two choices, you can accept it, in which case, you will retain minority ownership of the UEW, and get a healthy retirement fund contribution. It even gives you a position on the executive committee. It does however remove any authority you have in the areas of booking, and decision making. If you should choose to decline this offer, then you are fired. It's your choice. (Steele flips through the contract for a few minutes, then hands it over to Mike Haynes. His head drops, and he walks slowly to the back. As he heads to the back, the 70,000 plus fans in the Tokyo Dome simultaneously rise to their feet to give him a standing ovation. In fact, many of the UEW wrestlers come out from the back, and slap him on the back or shake his hand as he walks by.) Colby: Jay, you were the best in your time. But, time passed you by. Now it is time for a new generation. I just hope the next president can fulfill the goals I have for the UEW. (Colby pulls out another contract from his briefcase and hands it to Mike Haynes.) Colby: I'll save you the reading. That contract makes you UEW president until the conclusion of Ides of March. At that time, I will review your contract status. You have that much time to clean up the UEW, or you are fired as well. Now then, (turns to Don Thomas) You will no longer be needed here. You are here by terminated. Mike, your first order of business is to select me a new chief of security. I suggest you pick someone who can do something about the problems that plague the UEW. That just leaves me with one order of business. (He points at the Ultimate title and Mike Haynes brings it to him.) A lot of damage has been done here tonight. Everyone and their brother has been screwed over here tonight, and it stops here. As a result of a disqualification, the winner of this match is the Ladykiller Chris Tyler. Unfortunately, as vice-president, I do not have the authority to force the title to change hands on a dq. So, I am ordering a rematch. At the earliest possible date that I can get both parties to agree to, their will be a rematch for this title. (Colby steps over to Styles. The two men stare at one another for several moments.) Colby: Rick, you were the better man tonight. You outsmarted everyone. Congratulations champ. Next time... there will be no interference... you can take that to the bank. (Colby hands over the title to Styles as the closing credits roll. Fade to black.)
